# Skills · C · part 201

865 entries, conexus-sankhya to config-diff. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **conexus-sankhya**: Investigates a company's Sankhya before reading it or writing code that reads it. Sankhya is configured per company, so the skill teaches how to find what each code and field means in this company's data, what to ask the person, and how to record each proven mapping in the Project memory. Also covers the request format, the two read services, paging and the generated reader the app handler calls. ([developmentconexus-ops/conexus-os](https://github.com/developmentconexus-ops/conexus-os/tree/HEAD/builder-skills/conexus-sankhya))
- **conexus-server**: How an app saves data and runs logic under `conexus/`. Covers operations in `manifest.json`, handlers, reading a Conexão with `connectors.fetch`, tables and migrations, and failures. Use before designing an app's tables or changing anything in `conexus/`. ([developmentconexus-ops/conexus-os](https://github.com/developmentconexus-ops/conexus-os/tree/HEAD/builder-skills/conexus-server))
- **conexus-spec**: Write the spec of a Conexus wave from its approved study: an index.md the builders read, with a compiled code shape and one card per unit, and a rationale.md with the why.
